# Knight FM — Shared Worklog

Project: Knight Football Manager (Knight FM) — persistent multiplayer online football-management world.
Spec source: `/home/z/my-project/upload/Knight_FM_Master_Specification_v2.3_EN.docx` + Universal AI Full-Stack Prompt v2.3.
Platform constraint (environment): Next.js 16 App Router + TypeScript + Prisma/SQLite + shadcn/ui. Single user-visible route `/` (SPA views). APIs live under `/src/app/api/**`. Server-side SDK `z-ai-web-dev-sdk` for the Knight Assistant (backend only).

## Immutable invariants (spec §44) — enforced everywhere
1. Server authority for time/money/ownership/player/competition/match/permissions.
2. One game day = 24 exact real hours; UTC-only clock; rollover 00:00:00.000 UTC; GameDay = 1 + floor((UTCNow − WORLD_EPOCH)/24h).
3. No manual world progression / no advance-day / no skip-match endpoints anywhere.
4. Automatic jobs are idempotent (deterministic idempotency keys), recoverable, observable.
5. Financial mutations: atomic, integer-based, idempotent, audited; club treasury and personal wallet are separate ledgers.
6. Accepted manager contract: fixed total amount + exact real-day duration; daily salary derived once (Total ÷ Days) and never recomputed.
7. AI Assistant: read/explain/analyze only — can never execute market/bidding/ownership/treasury/deposit/withdrawal actions.
8. Owner max 10 clubs, never two in one region (DB + server enforced).
9. Squad capacity computed server-side by ICP entitlement.
10. 10% levy on incoming user funds (gross/levy/net reconciled entries); 5% regional + 5% system for eligible revenues.
11. Deposits: ≥10 meaningful server-side validation checks + anti-replay; wallet validation cryptographic Solana-specific.
12. Five themes, WCAG AA; four languages (ES primary, EN, FR, PT) — no hardcoded translatable strings.

## Architecture decisions (also in /docs/decisions/)
- D-001 Stack mapping: spec's Django/Postgres/React stack is mapped to the sandbox-mandated Next.js 16 (App Router, API routes), Prisma+SQLite, React 19 SPA inside single `/` route. Domain logic in `src/lib/engine/**`, thin API handlers.
- D-002 Scheduler: durable job ledger table `JobRun` with deterministic idempotency keys (e.g. `ROLLOVER:<season>:<day>`, `SALARY:<club>:<day>`, `MATCH:<fixtureId>`). Catch-up scheduler `src/lib/engine/scheduler.ts` runs opportunistically (world-state + scheduler tick endpoints) under an in-process lock; DB unique key prevents duplicates. UTC only.
- D-003 Auth: JWT access token (15 min) + rotating refresh token (7 d) via `jose`; sessions table stores hashed refresh tokens; logout revokes. Session kept in `sessionStorage` client-side only.
- D-004 CAPTCHA: Cloudflare Turnstile server-side (siteverify) when `TURNSTILE_SECRET_KEY` is configured (`CAPTCHA_PROVIDER=cloudflare_turnstile`). Sandbox default `CAPTCHA_PROVIDER=sandbox` records an audit event and requires the client token to be present, but performs no external call (no network secret available). Production must set the real provider.
- D-005 Email: `EMAIL_PROVIDER=console` in sandbox writes emails (verification codes, reset links) to dev.log + AuditEvent, never returned in API responses. SMTP provider interface ready.
- D-006 Password hashing: Argon2id via `@node-rs/argon2`.
- D-007 Match engine KMIE: causal, seeded, reproducible (seed = sha256(season:fixture:home:away)); strength from ContextualOVR of lineups + tactics fit; no rubber-banding; no scripted outcomes.
- D-008 Competitions calendar (spec-conflict resolution, documented): League = double round-robin, 30 rounds, days 1–28 (rounds 1–15 on days 1–15; rounds 16–30 compressed on days 16–28, two rounds on days 21 & 24). Kickoff 19:00 UTC (configurable). Regional cup knockout: rounds days 3,6,9,12,15,18,21,24,26,28, **final day 29**. Club World Championship: 40 clubs (top-3 of each region's 1st Division = 30; 10 regional cup winners; pass-down rule to 4th place), league phase 5 rounds + knockout compressed on day 30 with staggered UTC kickoffs (event-timestamp driven per spec §16). Season = 32 competitive days + 5 pre-season days (days 33–37); transition window 23:55–00:10 UTC.
- D-009 Presence: heartbeat API (60 s) + 5-minute window counts; polling gadget (WebSocket replaced by heartbeat in this environment; documented).
- D-010 Owner path economics: system club price is admin-configured (`economy.systemClubPrice`, default 100 $Knight). New users start with 0 personal funds; referral bonus (100) or real verified Solana deposit are the only legitimate funding routes. No fake balances anywhere; deposit verification requires configured RPC (else honest 503).
- D-011 SEO: single-route constraint → metadata/OG/JSON-LD provided via layout + landing; sitemap/robots endpoints deferred (documented).

## File ownership map (for parallel agents — do not touch files you don't own)
- OWNER (lead): `prisma/schema.prisma`, `src/lib/**` (core), `src/app/layout.tsx`, `src/app/globals.css`, `src/app/page.tsx` (final integration), `scripts/seed.ts`, `docs/decisions/*`.
- 2-a Auth: `src/app/api/auth/**`, `src/app/api/onboarding/**`, `src/components/auth/**`, i18n namespace `auth.*`
- 2-b Landing: `src/components/landing/**`, i18n namespace `landing.*`, analytics helper usage
- 3-a Game core APIs: `src/app/api/world/**`, `club/**`, `players/**`, `tactics/**`, `training/**`, `facilities/**`, `youth/**`, `competitions/**`
- 3-b Markets/finance APIs: `src/app/api/markets/**`, `treasury/**`, `wallet/**`, `messages/**`, `admin/**`, `assistant/**`
- 4-a Game shell UI: `src/components/game/shell.tsx`, `dashboard/**`, `squad/**`, `competitions/**`, `training/**`, `facilities/**`, `youth/**`, `onboarding/**`, `settings`
- 4-b Tactics UI: `src/components/game/tactics/**`
- 4-c Markets/treasury/assistant/admin UI: `src/components/game/markets/**`, `treasury/**`, `assistant/**`, `inbox/**`, `control-center/**`

Task ID: 2-a
Agent: auth-agent (Z.ai Code)
Task: Knight FM — Authentication + Onboarding, API + UI

Work Log:
- Read worklog.md invariants/decisions, core libs (types, api, auth, config, i18n, engine/clock, engine/finance) and prisma models before coding.
- i18n namespace `auth.*`: src/lib/i18n/dict/auth/{es,en,fr,pt}.ts (ES primary, full parity across all 4; incl. password-policy messages reused as API error i18n keys) + index.ts registering via registerNamespaces.
- Auth APIs (all use ok/fail/audit/rateLimit/clientIp/readJson + Zod v4; anti-enumeration generic errors; audit on every sensitive action):
  - POST /api/auth/register — rate 10/h/IP, captcha→402, password policy server-side (message = i18n key), case-insensitive email+username uniqueness (409 REGISTER_CONFLICT + AUTH_REGISTER_DUPLICATE audit), user + wallet(balance 0) transactional, 6-digit EMAIL_VERIFY token (sha256, 30 min), console email (D-005), optional referral binding (by username or id), AUTH_REGISTER.
  - POST /api/auth/verify-email — latest unconsumed token match by sha256(code), atomic consume (updateMany consumedAt:null guard), sets emailVerified, settles referral bonuses idempotently (grantReferralBonuses), EMAIL_VERIFIED. Reuse/wrong code → generic 400 TOKEN_INVALID.
  - POST /api/auth/resend-verification — 3/h per email+IP, generic {sent:true} regardless of existence/verified state, reissues token + console email only when applicable.
  - POST /api/auth/login — captcha→402, lockout key email|ip (checkLockout→429 LOCKED w/ retryAfterSec), bad creds → recordFailedLogin + 401 INVALID_CREDENTIALS, unverified → 403 EMAIL_NOT_VERIFIED (only after correct password), success → clearLockout + access(15m)/refresh(7d session) + AUTH_LOGIN + user payload {id, username, email, role, path, locale, theme, emailVerified, walletBalance, ownedClubIds, managedClubId}.
  - POST /api/auth/refresh — rotateRefreshToken, fresh access token signed with current role, old token single-use (401 on replay).
  - POST /api/auth/logout — revokes refresh token, AUTH_LOGOUT, always ok (idempotent).
  - GET /api/auth/me — profile with wallet.balance, clubsOwned[] (brand+region/division), managedClub with active contract {dailySalary, endDay, state}, unreadNotifications.
  - POST /api/auth/password-reset/request — 3/h per email+IP + 10/h per IP, generic {sent:true}, 48-hex token (sha256, 30 min), plaintext token ONLY in console email, PASSWORD_RESET_REQUESTED.
  - POST /api/auth/password-reset/confirm — policy checked BEFORE burning token, atomic consume, password updated, revokeAllSessions, PASSWORD_RESET_COMPLETED.
- Onboarding APIs:
  - GET /api/onboarding/state — {path, hasManagedClub, hasOwnedClub, needsPath, contractPreview:null}.
  - POST /api/onboarding/path — one-time MANAGER|OWNER (409 PATH_LOCKED if changed/club exists), ONBOARDING_PATH audit.
  - GET /api/onboarding/clubs?path&regionIndex&q&page — MANAGER: systemOwned+managerId null; OWNER: all systemOwned + price=economy.systemClubPrice(100). 24/page, total+pages, brand colors/initials, region/division index, avgOvr (rounded, non-youth non-retired), squadSize, facilitiesCount, operatingFund. Sorted region→division→name.
  - POST /api/onboarding/contract-preview — MANAGER-only; seasons=1, total=140 (config), durationDays = max(1, seasonEnd(=start+32+5-1) − gameDay + 1) via engine/clock, dailySalary = floor(total/days). Full breakdown returned (spec §14 display before acceptance).
  - POST /api/onboarding/accept-manager-contract — transactional re-check + atomic claim (club.updateMany where systemOwned && managerId null → 409 CLUB_TAKEN on race), single-ACTIVE-contract rule (409 CONTRACT_EXISTS), contract {totalAmount, durationDays, dailySalary, startDay=gameDay, endDay}, PermissionGrant {tactics,training,youth:true,transfers:false}, MANAGER_CONTRACT_ACCEPTED audit.
  - POST /api/onboarding/purchase-club — OWNER-only; max 10 clubs + max 1 per region enforced in-transaction (409 OWNER_CLUB_LIMIT / OWNER_REGION_LIMIT); 402 INSUFFICIENT_FUNDS with honest need/have message (no free money, D-010); atomic claim → debitPersonal(idem OWNER-CLUB:<uid>:<cid>, PURCHASE_CLUB) → creditFund SYSTEM (idem …:SYSTEM) → OwnershipRecord(day=gameDay) → CLUB_PURCHASED audit.
- Client (src/components/auth/):
  - store.ts — zustand useAuth {user, accessToken, refreshToken, status loading|anon|authed, init, login, logout, refresh, setUser, clear}; tokens in sessionStorage ONLY (key knightfm.auth); single-flight rotation on boot; apiFetch adds Bearer, unwraps {ok,data}/{ok,error}, ONE refresh+retry on 401 (skips for refresh/logout paths), typed ApiError {code, message, status, extra}.
  - captcha.ts — getCaptchaToken(): "sandbox-knight-token" when NEXT_PUBLIC_TURNSTILE_SITE_KEY absent; otherwise minimal explicit-render Turnstile loader (dark, invisible container), documented D-004.
  - AuthFlow.tsx — view machine login|register|verify|forgot|reset; shadcn Card/Tabs/Input/Label/Button/Select + useToast; live password policy checklist (12+/upper/lower/digit/symbol); optional referral field; verify view with honest sandbox hint (code only in server logs); after verify → auto-login; language selector (LANGUAGES) + theme selector (THEMES/useTheme) in panel header; responsive single column max-w-md, premium dark emerald styling, framer-motion fade-in, a11y labels/roles/sr-only. Default-exported; useAuth/apiFetch re-exported for convenience.
- Runtime curl evidence (server :3000, all well-formed {ok,…} envelopes):
  - register missing captcha → 402 CAPTCHA_INVALID; weak password → 400 PASSWORD_POLICY ("auth.err.passwordShort"); valid → 200 {email}; duplicate email w/ different case → 409; duplicate username w/ different case → 409.
  - login missing captcha → 402; wrong password → 401 INVALID_CREDENTIALS; unverified → 403 EMAIL_NOT_VERIFIED; verified → 200 with tokens + user.
  - verify-email with code read from dev.log console-email → 200 verified:true; code reuse → 400 TOKEN_INVALID.
  - /me with Bearer → 200 profile (wallet.balance 100 after referral); without → 401.
  - refresh → rotated tokens; replayed old refresh → 401 INVALID_REFRESH_TOKEN. logout → 200 done (idempotent).
  - onboarding: state → needsPath true; accept before path → 409 PATH_REQUIRED; path MANAGER → 200; clubs MANAGER → total 1600, 24/page (67 pages), avgOvr/squad/facilities present; q=Cumbre → 56; contract-preview → {totalAmount:140, durationDays:37, dailySalary:3, startDay:1, endDay:37}; accept → 200 ACTIVE contract; second club → 409 CONTRACT_EXISTS; owner accepting → 403 NOT_MANAGER.
  - owner flow: user3 (no referral, wallet 0) purchase → 402 INSUFFICIENT_FUNDS (need 100, have 0); user4 (referral, wallet 100) purchase → 200 {price:100, balanceAfter:0}; same-region second → 409 OWNER_REGION_LIMIT; DB check: OwnershipRecord(day=1), SYSTEM fund 100, ledger PERSONAL −100 + SYSTEM_FUND +100 with deterministic idem keys.
  - password reset: request → generic sent; confirm weak → 400 PASSWORD_POLICY (token preserved); confirm strong → 200; reuse → 400; login with new password → 200. DB: REFERRAL ledger entries with keys REFERRAL_BONUS:<referee> and REFERRAL_BONUS:<referee>:REFERRER (referrer re-payable across multiple referees).
- npx tsc --noEmit: zero errors in owned files (src/app/api/auth, src/app/api/onboarding, src/components/auth, src/lib/i18n/dict/auth). eslint on those paths: clean.

Stage Summary:
- Endpoints built: /api/auth/{register, verify-email, resend-verification, login, refresh, logout, me, password-reset/request, password-reset/confirm} + /api/onboarding/{state, path, clubs, contract-preview, accept-manager-contract, purchase-club}.
- Components: src/components/auth/{AuthFlow.tsx, store.ts, captcha.ts}; i18n namespace src/lib/i18n/dict/auth (index registers es/en/fr/pt).
- Decisions: referral idem keys derived per referee event (REFERRAL_BONUS:<refereeId> / REFERRAL_BONUS:<refereeId>:REFERRER) so a referrer is paid once per verified referee while staying replay-safe; referral bonus paid flat (no levy — platform-issued, not incoming user funds); register/login schemas accept missing captchaToken so verifyCaptcha yields 402 CAPTCHA_INVALID; club ownership claim via conditional updateMany inside transactions (SQLite-safe concurrency); created personalWallet(balance 0) at registration.
- BUG FOR LEAD (src/lib/auth.ts, not my file): checkLockout treats any recorded failure as locked (`rec.count >= 0` is always true) → a SINGLE failed login locks email|ip for 15 min, contradicting spec AUTH-09 (lock after security.loginLockoutAttempts=5). Suggested fix: only lock when rec.count >= maxAttempts. Test user test2a-1 is currently locked until the in-memory window expires.
- Test users created during validation: tester2a1 (unverified, referrer), tester2a2 (MANAGER of "CD Cumbre 1927"), tester2a3 (OWNER path, wallet 0 after reset flow), tester2a4 (OWNER of a region-5 club). Passwords are test-only (SuperSecure#2026x / BrandNew#2026pass).

Stage Summary:
- ENDPOINTS (all return {ok:true,data} / {ok:false,error:{code,message}}):
  Markets: GET /api/markets/direct (public, position/maxValue/page) · POST /api/markets/direct/list (owner, floor=ceil(mv×directSaleFloorPct/100)→400 BELOW_FLOOR) · POST /api/markets/direct/[id]/buy (atomic sale, idem TXBUY/TXSELL/TXALLOC:<listingId>, audit TRANSFER_COMPLETED) · GET /api/markets/auctions (lazy-closes due auctions first) · POST /api/markets/auctions/create (floor=mv×auctionFloorPct default 20%; hours 24|48|72) · POST /api/markets/auctions/[id]/bid (min increment 5%, no escrow) · GET /api/markets/free-agents (position/minOvr/maxOvr/page) · POST /api/markets/free-agents/[id]/sign (fee=mv, idem FASIGN:<playerId>, 403 CAPACITY_FULL) · POST /api/markets/release-clause (cost=stored clause else 5×mv, idem RCLS:<playerId>, works on system clubs) · GET/POST /api/markets/manager-offers (durationDays=seasons×37, dailySalary=floor(total/days) derived once, offer expires in market.managerOfferDays; one live offer per club) · POST /api/markets/manager-offers/[id]/apply (MANAGER+verified; 409 ACTIVE_CONTRACT; direct replacement; PermissionGrant defaults training/tactics/youth/transfers=true, finance/facilities=false).
  Treasury: GET /api/treasury?clubId= (owner OR manager; fund/debt/finState/unpaidDays + last 50 ledger) · POST /api/treasury/invest (personal→club, idem INV:<user>:<club>:<day>, 402 if short) · POST /api/treasury/withdraw (club→personal, idem TWDR:…, 403 DEBT_BLOCK if ownerHasDebt OR club.debt>0 — invariant #21).
  Wallet: GET /api/wallet (balance, last 50 PERSONAL ledger, depositAddressConfigured, priceAvailable) · GET /api/wallet/price (honest {available:false,reason:PRICE_SOURCE_NOT_CONFIGURED}; optional configured URL fetch → PriceSnapshot priceX1000) · POST /api/wallet/deposit (503 DEPOSIT_UNAVAILABLE w/o RPC/mint/wallet; anti-replay via DepositRequest unique (userId,signature) + global CREDITED check → 409 DUPLICATE_DEPOSIT; RPC getTransaction jsonParsed/finalized; 11 checks (TX_FOUND, TX_SUCCESS, FINALIZED, MINT_MATCH, DEST_OWNER_MATCH, DEST_PLATFORM_TOKEN_ACCOUNT, AMOUNT_POSITIVE_INTEGER, BALANCE_DELTA_MATCH, SIGNATURE_FORMAT, NOT_PREVIOUSLY_CREDITED, BLOCKTIME_SANITY 7d); on pass applyUserFundsLevy → creditPersonal(net, DEP:<sig>, gross/levy/net meta) + creditFund SYSTEM levy + BlockchainTransaction + audit DEPOSIT_CREDITED; transient failures stay PENDING "up to 1 hour") · POST /api/wallet/withdraw (min getInt("solana.minWithdraw",50); bs58→32-byte address else 400 INVALID_SOLANA_ADDRESS; DEBT_BLOCK; debitPersonal WITHDRAWAL_CRYPTO RESERVE idem WDR:<user>:<day>; w/o RPC → release + 503 WITHDRAWAL_UNAVAILABLE; with RPC → NEEDS_SIGNING "queued for manual signing infrastructure").
  Messages: GET /api/messages?box=inbox|sent (threads+unread) · POST /api/messages (30/10min, no self, Notification MSG_RECEIVED, audit w/o content) · POST /api/messages/read · GET /api/messages/notifications (30) · POST /api/messages/notifications/read.
  Admin (role ADMIN else 403 FORBIDDEN): GET/PUT /api/admin/config (defaults+rows merge; locked→403 LOCKED_KEY; int min/max→400 OUT_OF_RANGE; Approval row APPLIED; invalidateConfig) · POST /api/admin/maintenance (SystemState) · GET /api/admin/jobs (100) · GET /api/admin/audit?type=&page= (50/pg) · GET /api/admin/analytics (30d groupBy name + landing funnel) · POST /api/admin/reset (two-step token 10 min; confirm → {accepted:true, executed:false, reason:REQUIRES_BACKUP_PIPELINE}, audited, consumes token).
  Assistant: POST /api/assistant (20/10min; HARD GUARD system prompt — read/explain/analyze only, must refuse execute-actions; cheap club+day context; ≤4 relevant KnowledgeArticles; ZAI SDK backend-only; AssistantEntry log; SDK failure → honest 503 ASSISTANT_UNAVAILABLE with knowledge-fallback text) · GET /api/assistant/history (20).
  Presence: POST /api/presence/heartbeat (upsert + online 5-min window) · GET /api/presence/summary (public; online/weekly/monthly/yearly distinct users from Presence∪AnalyticsEvent, counts only).
  Scheduler: POST /api/scheduler/tick (30/min/IP; processDueJobs with in-process lock) · GET /api/scheduler/status.
  Analytics: POST /api/analytics/collect (120/min/IP; event ≤64 chars + charset; PII scrub (email-ish keys/values → [redacted]); 204 No Content).
- DECISIONS:
  1) Auctions are NON-ESCROWED by design (per task §6): bids store no funds; at close the handler walks bids high→low and settles the first bidder whose club can cover the amount AND has a free squad slot (insolvent/full → bid.refunded=true + AUCTION_BID_SKIPPED audit, skip to next). "Refund outbid reservations" is therefore a flag-only no-op (no reservation was ever taken) — documented honestly. Close is claim-guarded (OPEN→SETTLING) so scheduler tick and market reads never double-settle; failed settlement rolls the claim back for retry.
  2) Manager replacement via offer apply is DIRECT: club's previous ACTIVE contract → TERMINATED, settledAmount 0, no compensation (dismissal-with-settlement DISMISSAL belongs to a dedicated owner flow, out of scope here); new contract startDay=currentGameDay, endDay=startDay+days−1; PermissionGrant defaults written.
  3) System-owned sellers never enrich virtual clubs: sale/release proceeds credit the SYSTEM fund (creditFund TRANSFER_IN), then 5%+5% allocateRevenue on top per revenue rules.
  4) Capacity (invariant #9) enforced on every incoming transfer (direct buy, auction settlement, free-agent sign, release clause) with 403 CAPACITY_FULL / auction skip.
  5) DepositRequest states = PENDING→CREDITED|REJECTED (task vocabulary; schema comment predates — kept, documented). Transient verification failures (tx not found yet, RPC unreachable, not finalized) stay PENDING and are retryable with the same signature; nothing is credited before all 11 checks pass; levy split gross/levy/net reconciled on the ledger row.
  6) Withdrawal honesty: no signing key exists in the sandbox → with RPC configured the request parks in NEEDS_SIGNING ("queued for manual signing infrastructure"); without RPC the reservation is released and 503 WITHDRAWAL_UNAVAILABLE returned — never fake success. Config check happens after reserve per the task's literal release path.
  7) Deterministic daily idempotency keys for invest/withdraw (INV/TWDR per user:club:day) and crypto withdrawals (WDR per user:day) — replayed requests are ledger no-ops, not double spends.
  8) Note for lead: `economy.userFundsLevyPct` is invariant-adjacent (§44 #10) but is NOT marked locked in CONFIG_DEFAULTS; admins can currently move it 0–50. Consider locking it if the 10% levy is meant to be immutable.
- CURL EVIDENCE (all against live :3000):
  · GET /api/markets/direct → {"ok":true,...,"items":[],"total":0} · GET /api/markets/free-agents?position=MF&minOvr=40 → ok, players (Yago Duarte ovr 83 …), total 40 FAs
  · GET /api/presence/summary → {"online":0,"weekly":0,...} → after 2 heartbeats {"online":2,"weekly":2,"monthly":2,"yearly":2} · GET /api/scheduler/status → {"running":false,...}
  · POST /api/analytics/collect → 204; stored props {"email":"[redacted]","section":"hero","nested":{"contact":"[redacted]"}} (PII scrub verified in DB)
  · GET /api/wallet|/api/messages|POST /api/wallet/deposit|GET /api/admin/config without token → 401; admin config with USER token → 403 FORBIDDEN
  · POST /api/wallet/deposit (valid Bearer) → 503 {"code":"DEPOSIT_UNAVAILABLE","message":"Deposit infrastructure is not configured (RPC, mint or system wallet missing). Nothing was verified or credited."}
  · POST /api/wallet/withdraw amount=10 → 400 BELOW_MIN_WITHDRAW; address "0OIl…" → 400 INVALID_SOLANA_ADDRESS; valid 32-byte b58 + 60 → 503 WITHDRAWAL_UNAVAILABLE and wallet stayed 100 with WITHDRAWAL_CRYPTO −60 RESERVE / +60 RELEASE ledger rows
  · POST /api/treasury/invest (wallet 0) → 402 INSUFFICIENT_FUNDS; withdraw 100 → 200 fund 900 wallet 100; repeat same day → no-op (idempotent, fund still 900); invest 100 back → fund 1000
  · POST /api/markets/release-clause (cheap clause 110) → 200 TRANSFERRED; GET /api/treasury ledger reconciles (1000−100+100−110−27 SALARY−79 FASIGN = 784)
  · direct list: price 100 on mv-307 GK → 400 BELOW_FLOOR(floor 307); price 307 → 200; GET /api/markets/direct shows listing; buy own → 400 OWN_CLUB
  · auction create hours=25 → 400; hours=48 startPrice 10 (floor 5) → 200; GET /api/markets/auctions lists it; bid by manager w/o club → 400 CLUB_REQUIRED; backdated auction lazy-closed on GET → closed:1, state EXPIRED (no bids)
  · manager offer (1 season, 200) → durationDays 37 dailySalary 5 OPEN; apply by non-MANAGER → 403 MANAGER_PATH_REQUIRED; apply by MANAGER with active contract → 409 ACTIVE_CONTRACT

Work Log:
- Root cause: sendEmail (src/lib/auth.ts) only had the console transport — codes were written to dev.log and never delivered to real mailboxes (D-005 sandbox behavior). Users registering with gmail.com addresses never received anything.
- Rewrote email delivery in src/lib/auth.ts: new EmailDeliveryResult {delivered, provider, error}; providers selected via EMAIL_PROVIDER env: "resend" (Resend HTTP API, RESEND_API_KEY/EMAIL_FROM, plain fetch, no dep), "smtp" (nodemailer dynamic import, SMTP_HOST/PORT/SECURE/USER/PASS), "console" (default). Real-provider failures: audited EMAIL_DELIVERY_FAILED, console fallback log (code never lost), delivered=false. HTML branded email bodies. Added emailProviderConfigured() helper. Installed nodemailer + @types/nodemailer.
- Console-mode honesty fallback: issueEmailVerification now returns {devCode} and issuePasswordReset {devToken} ONLY when the effective transport is console (no real provider). With resend/smtp the code/token is NEVER exposed via API.
- Routes: register (ok({email, devCode?})), resend-verification (devCode only for existing unverified accounts; verified/unknown stays generic — anti-enumeration preserved), password-reset/request (devToken for existing users).
- UI (AuthFlow.tsx): devCode/devToken state; amber role=status banners (auth.verify.devBanner / auth.reset.devBanner) shown on verify/reset screens with the code/token in monospace; code/token auto-filled into inputs on register, resend and forgot; cleared after success. i18n keys added to es/en/fr/pt (full parity).
- .env: documented EMAIL_PROVIDER/RESEND_API_KEY/SMTP_*/EMAIL_FROM template (commented).
- Validation: tsc --noEmit → 0 errors in src/ (only pre-existing skills/ script error remains, out of scope); eslint on all touched dirs → clean. curl: register→devCode 144786→verify OK; password-reset request→devToken→confirm→login with new password OK; resend unverified→new devCode; resend verified→generic {sent:true} no devCode. Browser E2E: register uitest_fm → amber banner rendered with code 537291 (agent-ctx/final-check-devcode-banner.png) → auto-filled → Verificar cuenta → auto-login → "Elige tu camino". Zero runtime errors in dev.log.

- Audit: the deposit API (src/app/api/wallet/deposit/route.ts) already credits 100% gross (levy=0, Wave 9-a policy), and the wallet view already says "los depósitos no sufren gravamen". The liars were (a) game.onb.fundsDeposit in the onboarding FundsDialog and (b) landing.economy.levyDesc ("Sobre todos los fondos entrantes de usuarios").
- i18n fixes ×4 languages (es/en/fr/pt): game.onb.fundsDeposit now reads "Realiza un depósito verificado en Solana desde la Cartera: se acredita el 100%, sin gravamen."; landing.economy.levyDesc now scopes the 10% levy to platform incomes (manager salary, referral bonus) and withdrawals, explicitly exempting verified Solana deposits. Added game.onb.fundsDepositCta ("Ir a depositar ahora") and game.shell.walletOnlyBanner.
- Deposit escape hatch (new, mirrors the Task-15 admin ccOnly pattern): sessionStorage key kfm.onb.wallet + reactive zustand flag depositEscape in view-store. AuthedGate skips OnboardingFlow when set; GameShell derives walletOnly (flag && clubs.length===0), which takes PRECEDENCE over admin ccOnly (user's last explicit intent wins): restricted nav (Cartera only) + emerald banner with a "Elegir camino / club" return button + wallet module rendered directly. Flag auto-cleared once clubs exist; inbox button hidden; MobileNav/Sidebar pass both mode props.

- ROOT-CAUSE BUG found while wiring the live transition: queryClient.invalidateQueries(onboardingState) does NOT re-render AuthedGate when the refetched payload is identical — TanStack Query structural sharing keeps the same data reference, so no notification fires. Solved with the reactive zustand flag (initialized from sessionStorage so reloads persist the mode); goDeposit calls useViewStore.getState().setDepositEscape(true) for an immediate switch, no reload needed.
- Fixed pre-existing bug found on the way: MobileNav never passed ccOnly down to NavList, so mobile drawer showed the full nav in admin cc-only mode; also hooks-order lint fix (depositEscape subscription moved above AuthedGate's early returns).
- Verification (browser E2E as duenoknight — ADMIN, OWNER path, 0 balance, exactly the user's screenshot state): dialog shows new levy-free text + green CTA; click "Ir a depositar ahora" → LIVE transition into wallet-only shell (banner, restricted nav, full Cartera with levy-free deposit preview); "Elegir camino / club" → LIVE return to onboarding clubs step; reload with flag → wallet-only mode directly (persistence); ADMIN no longer trapped in ccOnly when depositing. Screenshots: t16-funds-dialog.png, t16-wallet-mode.png, t16-wallet-live-transition.png, t16-wallet-mobile.png. eslint clean; tsc 0 errors under src/; dev.log clean (wallet/club/mine/notifications all 200).

Confirmed live: unauth POST → UNAUTHORIZED; DB had only stale test rows.

Heartbeat with Bearer → 200 {online:2}; /api/presence/summary → online 2, weekly/monthly/yearly 4. Audit ONBOARDING_PATH rows carry previousPath + changed flag.

Work Log:
- New shared pure module src/lib/staff-quality.ts: STAR_BANDS (1★ 20-39, 2★ 40-54, 3★ 55-69, 4★ 70-84, 5★ 85-92), starsFor(quality), qualityCeilingFor(facilityLevel)=min(92,47+4·L) (same physics as the old generator), maxStarsForFacility, minFacilityLevelForStars (1★→L1, 2★→L1, 3★→L2, 4★→L6, 5★→L10), starRequirements map. No server imports → usable by routes AND client.
- /api/staff/route.ts rewritten: candidatesForRole(clubId, role, STARS, day, facilityLevel) now draws quality INSIDE the star's band (capped by facility ceiling; band fully above ceiling → []), deterministic seed `STAFFCAND:{club}:{role}:{stars}:{day}`, ids `cand-{role}-{stars}-{i}` (3 candidates per star level, daily rotation preserved); fee=quality×hireCostPerQuality, salary=quality/8 unchanged. GET response no longer returns the flat candidate pools — instead `areas` meta per role: facilityType, facilityLevel, maxStars, starRequirements (client renders the lock UI from this, no client/server formula duplication).

Stage Summary:
- Staff hiring is now exactly the flow the user asked for: FIRST choose the member's star quality (locked levels clearly show the facility requirement), THEN choose the person from that star's daily candidate pool.

- Duplicate protection: hired persons leave the day's pool and cannot be re-hired (API guard + UI filter); daily rotation kept deterministic per (club, role, stars, day).

- Loans always run to the last day of the current season — the client cannot choose a duration anymore.

Task ID: 24-c
Agent: security-agent (24-c)
Task: Capa perimetral anti-hacking — middleware de seguridad (headers + rate limit perimetral), security.txt RFC 9116, sanitización de texto libre, auditoría de guardas admin e higiene (cookies/fetch/dangerouslySetInnerHTML).

Work Log:
- NEW src/middleware.ts (edge, AUTOCONTENIDO — cero imports de src/lib/db, api o Prisma; solo lee headers, nunca bodies → SSE/presence y POSTs intactos, sin lógica de auth): (1) SECURITY HEADERS en TODAS las respuestas (next() y 429): CSP exacta (default-src 'self'; script-src 'self' 'unsafe-inline' 'unsafe-eval'; style-src 'self' 'unsafe-inline'; img-src 'self' data: blob:; font-src 'self' data:; connect-src 'self' ws: wss:; object-src 'none'; base-uri 'self'; form-action 'self'; frame-ancestors 'none' — sin upgrade-insecure-requests, preview http), X-Frame-Options DENY, X-Content-Type-Options nosniff, Referrer-Policy strict-origin-when-cross-origin, Permissions-Policy camera/microphone/geolocation/payment/usb=(), X-DNS-Prefetch-Control off, COOP same-origin; X-Robots-Tag "noindex, nofollow" SOLO si pathname empieza por /api/; Strict-Transport-Security "max-age=63072000; includeSubDomains" SOLO si x-forwarded-proto === "https". (2) RATE LIMIT perimetral con Map local (sliding window): bucket global por IP RATE_LIMIT_GLOBAL_MAX (env, default 300)/60s + bucket auth por IP para /api/auth/* con RATE_LIMIT_AUTH_MAX (env, default 60)/300s; cleanup perezoso por bucket (filtra vencidos) + sweep pasivo máx. 1/60s (elimina buckets sin hits en >300s) + CAP 10_000 claves con evict de la más vieja (Map insertion order) contra floods con XFF spoofeado; excedido → 429 JSON {"error":{"code":"RATE_LIMITED","message":"Too many requests"}} + Retry-After + headers de seguridad; X-RateLimit-Limit/Remaining en TODAS las respuestas API (bucket auth en /api/auth/*, global en el resto; remaining 0 en el 429). IP key = primer valor de x-forwarded-for (trim, slice 64) o "local"; todo el handler envuelto en try/catch (devuelve next() ante cualquier entrada malformada — nunca tumba la app). Matcher '/((?!_next/static|_next/image|favicon.ico|icon.png|apple-icon.png|robots.txt|sitemap.xml|manifest.webmanifest|knight-logo.png|og-knight-fm.png).*)' (excluye también el og-knight-fm.png del 24-b).
- next.config.ts: + poweredByHeader:false (conservado output standalone, ignoreBuildErrors, reactStrictMode). X-Powered-By ya no aparece en ninguna respuesta.
- NEW src/app/.well-known/security.txt/route.ts — GET estático text/plain;charset=utf-8 RFC 9116: Contact: mailto:security@knightfm.game, Expires = ISO-8601 UTC a +1 año, Preferred-Languages: es, en, fr, pt, Canonical = (NEXT_PUBLIC_SITE_URL || http://localhost:3000) + "/.well-known/security.txt".
- NEW src/lib/security.ts: sanitizeText(input, maxLen) — elimina C0 (U+0000–1F), DEL y C1 (U+007F–9F), colapsa runs de whitespace, trim, corte duro a maxLen code points (emoji/UTF-8 preservados; defensiva ante no-string); escapeHtml(s) (& < > " ') para sinks no-React.
- src/app/api/messages/route.ts (POST): sanitiza el cuerpo del mensaje con sanitizeText(body, 4000) tras las validaciones zod intactas (min1/max4000) y antes de persistir; guardia nueva: si queda vacío tras sanitizar → 400 BAD_REQUEST "Message body cannot be empty"; contrato de respuesta {messageId, sentAt} sin cambios; GET/audit sin tocar (audit ya excluye contenido por privacidad). Otras rutas con texto libre detectadas y NO tocadas (fuera de mi alcance o admin-guardadas): /api/assistant (content 1–4000, recomendado aplicar sanitizeText), /api/admin/maintenance (message ≤300, solo ADMIN), /api/admin/funds (memo ≤200, solo ADMIN).
- AUDITORÍA GUARDAS ADMIN (read-only): 9/9 rutas en src/app/api/admin/** (analytics, maintenance, promote, config, audit, reset, users, jobs, funds) usan requireAuth + `if (auth.role !== "ADMIN") return fail("FORBIDDEN", "Admin access required", 403)` — ninguna sin guarda, cero fixes necesarios.
- HIGIENE: rg "cookies(" en src → 0 matches (la app es 100% Bearer headers; no hay cookies propias que endurecer); rg fetch( con URLs absolutas http(s) en src/components → 0 matches (todas relativas); dangerouslySetInnerHTML solo 3: layout.tsx themeBoot (script estático, seguro) + JSON-LD estático (seguro) y src/components/ui/chart.tsx (ChartStyle de shadcn: inyecta CSS variables del ChartConfig del desarrollador, no contenido de usuario — seguro). Nada que corregir.

Work Log:
- NEW src/lib/vpn.ts: loadVpnConfig() (getConfig security.vpnPolicy + security.vpnDetectUrl); isIpInCidr(ip, cidr) — IPv4 exacto + CIDR /0../32 con máscara exacta, IPv6 exacto (parser propio con compresión "::", 8 grupos) y prefijos IPv6 best-effort (comparación de bits altos); isValidVpnValue/isExactIpv4/isExactIpv6 exportados para validar en el CRUD con el MISMO parser; isExcepted(ip) con cache en memoria 60s (Map<value,true>, una sola findMany por TTL); invalidateVpnCache() llamada desde POST/DELETE del CRUD; detectVpn(ip) — URL vacía → false (nunca hay bloqueo sin evidencia), GET `${url}?ip=` con AbortController 3s, JSON {vpn:boolean}, cualquier error/timeout → false (fail-open) + console.warn("[vpn] detector failed (fail-open)"); evaluateVpn(ip) → {flagged, excepted, blocked, policy} con blocked = policy empieza por "block" && flagged && !excepted. NOTA DOCUMENTADA: el valor viejo "block_except_cuba" (y cualquier "block_*") se trata como bloqueo pleno — la excepción Cuba (y cualquier otra) se expresa por la tabla VpnException.

LOGIN bypass ADMIN documentado en comentario: la evaluación corre igual pero el bloqueo se aplica tras verifyPassword (ahí se conoce el rol) — user.role !== "ADMIN" → 403; ADMIN → 200 + VPN_LOG con adminBypass:true. REGISTER y REFRESH bloquean SIEMPRE que blocked (sin bypass; en refresh evita canjear un refresh token robado desde un nodo de salida VPN).

ON CONFLICT(value) DO UPDATE, id randomUUID) cuando el cliente en memoria es viejo — las dos vías apuntan a la misma tabla y tras reiniciar mandan los delegates. Con el servidor actual (cliente regenerado) las pruebas corren por delegates (ids cuid en las respuestas).

Stage Summary:
- Accounts now age out on their own: no access for 40 days → INACTIVE; 60 days → hard DELETE with their clubs returned to the system under the ORIGINAL name, manager contracts terminated, personal data wiped (sessions/messages/wallet/presence), referrals detached — all inside transactions and audited; ADMIN accounts never expire. lastActiveAt refreshes centrally in requireAuth (6h throttle).
- Salaries (players + staff + manager, levies included) are paid every finance.salaryIntervalDays game days (default 7), amount = daily rate × interval, same idempotency keys.
- The Control Center (Mantenimiento) shows the world anchor and lets admins SET season-1 start date; re-anchoring a running world requires explicit force (409 SEASON_EXISTS otherwise) and is audited; from the chosen date the world advances automatically.
- Club income (prizes, player sales, matchday revenue, loan fees, auction sales) pays a configurable gravamen (economy.clubIncomeTaxPct, default 10%) to the SYSTEM fund inside the same transaction; owner deposits, auction refunds and admin grants are exempt. Auction flow migrated from a hardcoded levy to the configurable one.
- Free-agent pool size is admin-configurable (market.freeAgentPoolSize, default 40) with the existing 24h rotation proven to create fresh players every game day.
- Training gains now combine condition (100−fatigue), age, quality (ovr), average coach quality and training-center level via five admin-weighted factors (0 disables, 200 doubles), with per-session breakdown returned by the API and shown in the UI toast.
- VPN policy: log_only (default) or block; detection pluggable via security.vpnDetectUrl (JSON {"vpn":true}, 3s timeout, fail-open, always audited VPN_LOG/VPN_BLOCK); admins bypass on login; per-IP/CIDR exceptions with 60s cache are CRUD-managed from the Control Center (acceso tab) with full audit.

Stage Summary:
- Any manager can now open ANOTHER club's squad from the standings table or from a match report and exercise a rescission clause on any eligible player (not on-loan, not their own club) with the exact cost shown before confirming.
- Dismissing a player or staff member costs the REMAINING CONTRACT DAYS of salary (season-end contracts; staff multiplier configurable, default 1) and EVERY dismissed-contract fund goes to the SYSTEM fund as a paired, idempotent ledger entry — money never reaches another user.
- The cantera is capped by the youth academy: level 1 holds exactly 3 prospects (youth.baseCapacity) and every upgrade adds 2 more (youth.capacityPerLevel) up to the absolute ceiling (youth.maxProspectsPool, default 50) — scouting (manual and passive) fails with YOUTH_FULL when full and is trimmed to the remaining space otherwise; the UI shows the live usage badge.
- Every decided match pays a win prize to the winning club — differentiated league 80 / cup 120 / world 250 by default (competition.winPrize*, admin-editable, gravamen 10% included) — sized so a typical club covers its wage bill without accumulating wealth; friendlies and draws pay nothing.
- Engine hardening: failed MATCH jobs now retry instead of wedging the fixture forever, and the match transaction no longer times out on busy matchdays (5s→30s).

Work Log:
- src/app/api/admin/maintenance/route.ts (REWROTE): POST now upserts BOTH keys into the configKey table via db.configKey.upsert (registry metadata stamped from CONFIG_DEFAULTS: group/valueType/defaultValue/min/max/description, currentValue = value, updatedBy = admin) — "ops.maintenanceMode" = String(enabled), "ops.maintenanceMessage" only when provided — then invalidateConfig() so the 30s cache drops immediately. Same body {enabled, message? ≤300}, same MAINTENANCE_TOGGLED audit, same responses. The old SystemState writes are GONE (that was the bug: all readers read configKey). NEW admin-guarded GET returns ok({ enabled: getBool("ops.maintenanceMode"), message: getConfig("ops.maintenanceMessage") }).
- login route: maintenance gate placed AFTER password verification + VPN enforcement and BEFORE the emailVerified check — user.role !== "ADMIN" && getBool("ops.maintenanceMode") → fire-and-forget audit("AUTH_LOGIN_BLOCKED", user.id, {reason:"MAINTENANCE", ip}).catch + fail("MAINTENANCE", maintenanceMessage, 503). No account state leaks pre-auth; admins pass through untouched.
- register route: gate immediately after the initial rate-limit (before VPN guard / captcha / any DB work): maintenance on → fail("MAINTENANCE", message, 503) (no admin bypass needed — nobody can register during maintenance).
- refresh route: gate AFTER rotateRefreshToken + role re-read from DB, BEFORE signAccessToken — non-ADMIN under maintenance → fail("MAINTENANCE", message, 503) with NO access token signed (the already-rotated refresh token is simply not returned; the client clears auth and lands on the login screen which shows the maintenance message). ADMINs pass through.
- src/lib/api.ts requireAuth: after getAuth token verification, if auth.role !== "ADMIN" && getBool("ops.maintenanceMode") → fail("MAINTENANCE", maintenanceMessage, 503) returned BEFORE touchLastActive (blocked requests are not "access" for the inactivity engine). Contract unchanged (AuthCtx | NextResponse). Import of getBool/getConfig from @/lib/config creates no cycle (config.ts only imports db).
- Sanity grep "ops.maintenance" across src/: writer = only admin/maintenance (configKey now); readers = world/state, admin/config (registry), control-center MaintenanceTab (via /api/admin/config rows) — ALL unified on the config registry; ZERO maintenance reads of SystemState remain (other systemState keys like world.epoch/scheduler.lastDay/admin.resetToken are unrelated and untouched).
- src/lib/engine/inactivity.ts: deleteUserAccount EXPORTED with new signature (userId, email, day, actorId: string | null = null) → Promise<UserDeleteResult> where UserDeleteResult = { clubsReverted: string[] }; USER_DELETED audit actorId is now the parameter (null for the daily job, the deleting ADMIN for manual deletion). emailHash8 privacy hashing + payload shape unchanged. runInactivity call site untouched (still `await deleteUserAccount(user.id, user.email, day)` — return value ignored, compiles clean).
- NEW src/app/api/admin/users/[id]/route.ts (runtime nodejs, dynamic force-dynamic, Next 16 awaited params): standard ADMIN guard → load target {id,email,role} → 404 USER_NOT_FOUND / 403 ADMIN_DELETE_FORBIDDEN (target.role==="ADMIN"; also covers self-deletion) → day = await currentGameDay().catch(() => 0) → deleteUserAccount(id, target.email, day, auth.userId) in try/catch → success: ok({deleted:true, clubsReverted: res.clubsReverted}) (USER_DELETED audit fires inside the engine with the admin as actor — no duplicate audit added); error: fire-and-forget audit("USER_DELETE_FAILED", auth.userId, {userId, error:String(e)}) + fail("USER_DELETE_FAILED", "The account could not be deleted", 500). Rate limit SKIPPED: promote/funds (the referenced admin-route patterns) use none.
- Verified error codes match the pre-existing i18n keys: MAINTENANCE, USER_NOT_FOUND, ADMIN_DELETE_FORBIDDEN, USER_DELETE_FAILED (UI maps t(`game.err.${code}`) dynamically; i18n dicts untouched per instructions).
- Quality: bunx tsc --noEmit → 0 errors in src/ (only 4 pre-existing errors in examples/websocket/* and skills/* — module resolution outside the app, NOT mine); bun run lint → 0 problems; no test files created; dev server not run (dev.log tail checked read-only: only heartbeats, no compile errors).

Stage Summary:

ADMINs keep full access everywhere (they need the Control Center to switch it off).

- E2E browser (skill agent-browser, --session t27): login t10owner → Competiciones → new "Calendario" tab renders the season grid with legend, HOY pill on day 5, scores (t27-calendar-full.png), click Día 1 → Acta del partido opens (t27-calendar-match-dialog.png); Plantilla shows "Lesionado (8 d)", "Sancionado (1)/(2)" badges (values tripled: 2133/2145 $Knight) + detail dialog "Lesionado (2 d) hasta 26 sept 2026, 2:45" + "Sancionado (2)" (t27-player-detail-suspended.png); rival squad dialog badge "Lesionado (1 d)" (t27-rival-squad-badges.png); maintenance enabled → non-admin reload lands on landing, login attempt shows the configured message inline + toast (t27-login-maintenance-msg.png), admin login passes and Control Center accessible; delete user UI: search qa27_ui → AlertDialog (t27-delete-confirm.png) → confirm → toast "Cuenta eliminada" (t27-deleted-toast.png) + row gone from DB; mobile 390: drawer nav → calendar 2-col, no horizontal overflow (scrollWidth 390) (t27-calendar-mobile.png). Seeded QA statuses (2 suspensions) cleared after; redCardChancePct restored to 5; maintenance left DISABLED.
- Verification: bun run lint exit 0; bunx tsc --noEmit → 0 errors under src/ (pre-existing examples/skills only); dev.log recent window: only the expected 503s from the maintenance tests, zero 500s.

Stage Summary:
- League money now respects the pyramid: division-1 clubs earn the full configured win/title prizes and every division below earns a configurable step less (floor 10%) — promotions are now financially meaningful.
- Cup and world-cup winnings start minimal in early rounds and grow round by round; the FINAL winner collects exactly the configured champion prize under the same idempotency key as the season payout (double-pay impossible).
- Every player's VALUE is the system calculation × 3 (admin-configurable): transfer prices, clauses, listing floors and free-agent fees tripled, while salaries stay tied to the base value so the wage economy is untouched — stronger money sinks, same sustainability.
- Loaned players are paid by the HOST club for the whole loan (verified: host debited salary × interval, origin zero).
- Maintenance mode truly works now (configKey-backed): only admins can log in / refresh / call APIs; everyone else sees the configured maintenance message at login and a full-screen notice in-game.
- Admins can hard-delete user accounts from the Control Center with a two-step confirm (clubs revert to system, wallet erased, USER_DELETED audited); deleting admins is forbidden.
- Red cards exist (configurable chance + ban length) with the ban count shown on player cards everywhere and RED_CARD events in match reports; injuries show remaining recovery days.
- The new season Calendar tab maps all 37 season days with per-competition color identity, today marker, scores and match reports — on desktop and mobile.

Task ID: 28
Agent: lead (Z.ai Code)
Task: User batch (Task 28) — (1) staggered match kickoffs per region so the scheduler never simulates a whole matchday at the same instant (server-load protection); (2) yellow-card probability, configurable; (3) accumulation rules: 2 yellows in ONE match = send-off (red), 5 yellows across DISTINCT matches = ONE-match ban.

Work Log:
- Schema: Player.yellowCards Int @default(0) — season accumulator of bookings (each MATCH counts once). db:push clean.
- Config registry +5 keys (Control Center total 107): matches.yellowCardChancePct (40, chance per DRAW), matches.yellowCardMaxPerMatch (4 independent draws/match), matches.yellowCardsForSuspension (5 bookings across distinct matches → 1-match ban, counter resets), competition.kickoffRegionStaggerMinutes (90 — each region shifts by its RANK among regions ordered by index × this), competition.kickoffMinuteJitter (20 — deterministic per-fixture minute jitter).
- Engine (fixtures.ts): new exported helpers regionKickoffOffsetMs (rank-based, cached 60s — raw index NEVER used: sentinel 99998 lands last), kickoffJitterMs (mulberry32 seeded from fixture identity — SAME seed key derivable at generation AND backfill time), fixtureKickoffDeltaMs. Applied to generateLeagueFixtures (division→region offset + per-fixture jitter) and generateCupInitial (round-1 17:00 + region offset + jitter).
- Engine (matches.ts): advanceCup round kickoffs get the same delta; World-cup LEAGUE phase now spreads each round's 20 pairs EVENLY across its hour (3-min steps) — no more 20-at-once bursts. DISCIPLINE block rewritten: single redCardPlayer → sendOffs Map + bookedIds Set. Straight red unchanged (chance, ban 1..N). Yellow draws: up to maxPerMatch independent draws; a player already booked and drawn again = SECOND_YELLOW send-off (RED_CARD event with detail "SECOND_YELLOW", same 1..N ban ladder, stacked with any threshold ban); a sent-off player cannot be booked; booked players get yellowCards+1 ONCE per match; reaching yellowCardsForSuspension adds a ONE-match ban served from the NEXT club fixture (max(0,susp-1)+1) and resets the counter to 0. Per-player tx loop writes yellowCards only for booked players (no full-table writes).
- Engine (scheduler.ts): seasonTransition step 3b resets every player's yellowCards to 0 for the new season.
- API: /api/club/[id] squad rows, /api/players rows and /api/players/[id] state now carry yellowCards.
- UI: squad-view card badges + player detail dialog + rival club-squad-dialog show an amber "Amarillas: {n}" chip (RectangleHorizontal icon, amber-400) whenever yellowCards > 0 — complements the existing "Sancionado ({n})" / "Lesionado ({d} d)" badges.
- i18n ×4: player.yellowCards (Amarillas/Yellows/Cartons jaunes/Amarelos) + 5 cfg.*.label entries in admincfg dicts.
- scripts/task28-backfill.ts: rewrote 21092 future SCHEDULED league/cup kickoffs adding rank×90min + identity jitter (108 skipped with delta 0; PLAYED fixtures untouched via minutes==0&&seconds==0 guard; SystemState marker task28.kickoffBackfill). Matchday spread now ~197 distinct kickoff slots over ~14h (19:00 → next-day 08:49 UTC).
- scripts/task28-verify.ts: 33/33 PASS — jitter determinism/bounds; regional offsets strictly increasing with rank (step = configured stagger); DETERMINISTIC sandbox: sole-eligible-player isolation → (c) 1 draw @100% books the 4/5-yellow player → suspension=1 served NEXT fixture + counter reset to 0; (d) 2 draws @100% on the same player → 1 YELLOW_CARD + 1 RED_CARD(detail SECOND_YELLOW), ban 1..2, tally counts the booking ONCE; (e) chance 0 → zero cards; (f) backfill audit: >80% of future fixtures off the exact hour, ≥5 distinct kickoff HOURS on the next matchday, all PLAYED kickoffs intact.
- QA hygiene: seeded 2 demo-club yellowCards for the browser check then reset to 0; disposable t28admin@knightfm.test (register→verify→role ADMIN) disposed FK-safe after testing; the 3 config rows created by the verify script had their defaultValue metadata corrected (40/4/5); kickoff keys live in the registry (DB rows created on first admin edit — GET merges registry+DB).
- curl: admin login → /api/admin/config lists the 5 new keys (total 107); /api/club/[id] squad carries yellowCards; fixtures API shows staggered kickoffs (19:09, 19:04, 19:12, 19:18, 22:17, 22:02…).
- E2E browser (skill agent-browser, isolated --session t28): login t10owner → Plantilla shows "Amarillas: 3" (Sergio Ferrer, alongside "Lesionado (8 d)") and "Amarillas: 1" (Lucas Haros) (t28-squad-yellow-badges.png); detail dialog shows the chip too (t28-player-detail-yellow.png); Partidos tab rows display per-fixture times with zero collisions (t28-fixtures-staggered.png); mobile 390: scrollWidth 390, no overflow (t28-fixtures-mobile.png). NOTE: calendar day cells intentionally do not render times (Task 27 design — times live in the match dialog and fixtures list); pending cells are not clickable by design.
- Verification: bun run lint exit 0; bunx tsc --noEmit → 0 errors under src/; dev.log after restart: only 200s, zero 500s (a transient 500 right after the schema push was the running server's stale Prisma client — fixed by dev-server restart, no code change).

Stage Summary:
- Matchdays no longer detonate at one instant: every region's fixtures are offset by rank×90min (configurable 0..240) plus a deterministic per-fixture 0..19-minute jitter, and the world-cup league rounds spread their 20 pairs evenly across each hour — the scheduler now processes a gentle stream instead of a 400-fixture burst, and the calendar/fixtures UI shows the real varied times.
- Discipline is now a full card system: configurable yellow-card probability (per-draw chance × max draws per match), 2 yellows in one match = send-off (SECOND_YELLOW red with the same ban ladder), and 5 yellows across distinct matches = a one-match ban that resets the counter — all visible on every player card as "Amarillas: {n}" and configurable from the Control Center.

Task: Restricción de selección de clubes — solo divisiones 5ª a la última seleccionables (nuevos registros, managers que abandonan un club y propietarios que compran más clubes). Los clubes de divisiones superiores (1ª–4ª) no aparecen como disponibles.

Work Log:
- Nueva clave de configuración world.pickMinDivisionIndex (grupo world, int, default 5, min 1, max 50) en src/lib/config.ts — Control Center total 108 claves. Describe la regla: solo divisiones con índice >= valor son seleccionables; las superiores quedan protegidas.
- GET /api/onboarding/clubs: where += division: { index: { gte: minDivisionIndex } } (aplica a AMBOS paths MANAGER/OWNER, cubre primer club, re-elección tras renuncia y compra adicional de propietario). Respuesta += minDivisionIndex para que la UI explique la regla. Verificado: 960 clubes (6 divisiones × 10 regiones × 16) con default 5; 640 con config 7.
- POST /api/onboarding/purchase-club: guarda CLUB_DIVISION_LOCKED 403 si club.division.index < min — EXCEPCIÓN dueño-operador: un manager que compra el club que YA dirige (club.managerId === userId) no pasa por el gate (no es una elección libre; ya lo dirige). El club no se toca (verificado: sigue systemOwned).
- POST /api/onboarding/accept-manager-contract: mismo gate dentro de la transacción, antes del claim atómico (cubre primer contrato y re-elección tras abandonar/resignar).
- POST /api/onboarding/contract-preview: mismo gate (CLUB_DIVISION_LOCKED 403) — el bloqueo es visible antes de aceptar.
- Alcance documentado: las ofertas de manager (POST /api/markets/manager-offers/[id]/apply) quedan FUERA del gate — son reclutamiento iniciado por el propietario de un club YA adquirido (no lista de selección libre); la división de un club propio cambia por ascensos/descensos.
- Frontend: api.ts OnboardingClubsResponse += minDivisionIndex; clubs-tab.tsx interfaz local += minDivisionIndex; aviso ámbar (Alert, Info icon) en el onboarding ClubsStep (game.onb.divisionRestrict con {n}) y en Mercados → Venta de clubes (markets.clubs.divisionRestrict con {n}).
- i18n ×4 paridad: game.err.CLUB_DIVISION_LOCKED, game.onb.divisionRestrict, markets.clubs.divisionRestrict, cfg.world.pickMinDivisionIndex.label.
- curl: purchase div-1 (Pradera CF) y div-4 → 403 CLUB_DIVISION_LOCKED, club intacto; accept-manager-contract div-1 → 403; contract-preview div-1 sin manager (Real Faro 1927) → 403; accept div-5 (Atlético Lago) → 200 (contrato creado y luego resign + club revertido a sistema); config PUT 7 → listado 640/División 7+; PUT 0 → 400 OUT_OF_RANGE; restore 5 → 960; clave visible en GET /api/admin/config (108 keys).
- E2E browser (skill agent-browser, sesiones t30/t30o): registro+login usuario fresco → Paso 1 → Mánager → Paso 2 "Elige tu club" muestra aviso ámbar "Clubes disponibles desde la 5ª división hasta la última…", tarjetas solo División 5/6 y "960 clubes disponibles" (t30-onboarding-clubs-restricted.png); móvil 390 sin overflow (t30-onboarding-mobile.png); t10owner → Mercados → Venta de clubes: aviso ámbar visible y tarjetas desde División 5 (el "División 1" del header es el club propio del usuario) (t30-markets-clubs-tab.png).

- Tres capas de enforcement: el listado no muestra clubes protegidos, la compra y la aceptación de contrato los rechazan con CLUB_DIVISION_LOCKED (403) aunque se salte la UI, y la vista previa del contrato también bloquea. Excepción única: el dueño-operador que compra el club que ya dirige.
- La regla es transparente: aviso informativo con el mínimo configurado en el onboarding y en Mercados → Venta de clubes, en los 4 idiomas.

Task ID: 33
Agent: lead (Z.ai Code)
Task: Mercado secundario de clubes — precio fijo SOLO para clubes del sistema; los clubes de propietarios se venden al precio libre que fije su dueño, con una opción dedicada para que los propietarios pongan sus clubes en venta.

Work Log:
- Regla de negocio implementada según el usuario: los clubes del sistema conservan el precio fijo (economy.systemClubPrice, con el gate de división world.pickMinDivisionIndex intacto); los clubes de propietarios entran en un mercado secundario con precio 100% libre fijado por el vendedor (1..2.000.000.000, int32-safe). La reventa queda exenta del gate de división por diseño: esos clubes llegaron a su división por mérito deportivo (el aviso ámbar ahora dice explícitamente "Clubes del sistema…").
- Prisma: Club += salePrice Int? + saleListedDay Int? (índice @@index([salePrice])); db:push OK (64 ms). El cliente Prisma obsoleto del dev server requirió reinicio (500 transitorio conocido).
- API nueva: /api/markets/clubs/sale — GET feed de listings de OTROS propietarios (excluye los propios, orden saleListedDay desc, cap 120, proyección completa plantel/media/instalaciones/fondo + vendedor + hasManager), POST publicar/actualizar precio (dueño-only, NOT_CLUB_OWNER 403, PRICE_OUT_OF_RANGE 400), DELETE retirar (?clubId=). Auditoría CLUB_SALE_LISTED / CLUB_SALE_UNLISTED.
- API nueva: POST /api/markets/clubs/sale/buy — compra atómica: claim updateMany (ownerId+salePrice not null) ANTES de mover dinero; transferencia directa cartera→cartera sin comisión (debitPersonal PURCHASE_CLUY / creditPersonal CLUB_SALE con saleRef UUID único en las claves de idempotencia — no reutilizables entre re-listados); OwnershipRecord (fromOwner/toOwner/amount/day); regla de mánager espejo de purchase-club (comprar otro club renuncia el actual; comprar el que dirigen los mantiene como propietario-director); límites §44.8 (10 clubes, 1 por región) para el comprador; contratos del club vendido: mánager contratado ajeno lo conserva y recibe notification.clubNewOwner; propietario-director vendedor ve su contrato RESIGNED + permissionGrant limpiados; vendedor recibe notification.clubSold (payload clubName/amount/day); ventana de renombrado limpia para el nuevo dueño (nameChangeDay null); errores: CLUB_NOT_FOR_SALE 409, OWN_CLUB_PURCHASE 409, INSUFFICIENT_FUNDS 402, PATH_REQUIRED 409. Auditoría CLUB_SOLD.
- GET /api/club/mine += salePrice/saleListedDay (la sección del vendedor lee del cache existente).
- UI Mercados → Venta de clubes reorganizada en 3 bloques: (1) "Mis clubes en venta" REAL (reemplaza el placeholder "no disponible": fila por club con escudo, región·división, badge En venta por {price}/No en venta, input de precio, Publicar/Actualizar precio/Retirar de la venta, estados de carga y toasts honestos); (2) "Clubes de propietarios en venta" (feed del comprador: tarjetas con badge "Precio del propietario", vendedor, precio libre, diálogo de confirmación con desglose; se oculta si no hay ofertas); (3) "Clubes del sistema en venta" (catálogo de precio fijo intacto con su encabezado propio).

El vendedor ha recibido el importe." (t33-buyer-bought.png); móvil 390 sin overflow (scrollWidth 390, t33-mobile-clubs.png). Task ID: 33-lead
Agent: lead (Z.ai Code)
Task: Gravamen del 10% sobre ingresos de usuarios + venta de clubes al 90% (regla del usuario) + coordinación del barrido visual Task 33 (33-v1/v2/v3).

Work Log:
- REGLA DEL USUARIO implementada: todos los ingresos de usuarios están gravados (default 10%, configurable por el administrador vía economy.userFundsLevyPct) y, cuando un propietario vende un club, se le cobra el 10% que va a los FONDOS DEL SISTEMA — el propietario recibe el 90% de la venta.
- src/lib/engine/finance.ts: applyUserFundsLevy ahora devuelve también pct (además de gross/levy/net) y acota el valor leído a 0..100. Sin cambios de comportamiento para sus llamadores existentes (salarios, referidos del scheduler).
- src/app/api/markets/clubs/sale/buy/route.ts (venta de reventa): el comprador paga el precio íntegro; el vendedor recibe el NETO vía creditPersonal con meta gross/levy/net reconciliada (invariante #10) y el gravamen se acredita al FONDO SYSTEM con creditFund bajo la clave `CLUB-SALE-SELL:<saleRef>:LEVY` dentro de la MISMA transacción. Respuesta += levy/netToSeller; auditoría CLUB_SOLD += levy/net; notificación clubSold += net + levyPct en el payload. Memo del ledger: "Venta del club X (neto tras gravamen del N%)".
- Consistencia de referidos corregida: grantReferralBonuses (src/app/api/auth/_shared.ts, camino verificación-email) AHORA aplica el mismo gravamen (applyUserFundsLevy + creditFund SYSTEM con clave determinista `REFERRAL_BONUS:<refereeId>:LEVY`) que ya aplicaba el scheduler settleReferrals — antes el bono llegaba íntegro o neto según qué camino liquidara el referido.
- Config: economy.userFundsLevyPct minValue 1→0 (el admin puede desactivarlo) y descripción actualizada (salarios, referidos y ventas de clubes; inversiones y depósitos exentos — decisión documentada en deposit route). El label admincfg ya existía en ×4 ("Gravamen sobre ingresos de usuarios (%)").
- GET /api/markets/clubs/sale += levyPct para que la UI muestre desgloses honestos (el servidor recalcula de forma autoritativa en la compra).
- UI (markets/tabs/clubs-tab.tsx): (1) pista bajo el input de precio del vendedor: "Recibirás ≈{net} (gravamen de ingresos del {pct} %: {levy} van a los fondos del sistema)" cuando el precio es válido; (2) diálogo de compra reescrito con desglose de 3 filas — Precio de venta / Gravamen de ingresos ({pct} %) en destructive / El vendedor recibe en primary con borde superior; (3) textos mySellDesc/buyOwnerDesc/buyOwnerSuccess actualizados a "importe neto".
- Inbox: NotificationRow formatea net/levy como moneda y añade fallback net←amount para notificaciones clubSold legacy (nunca muestra placeholders crudos).
- i18n ×4 (dict markets): 6 claves nuevas (clubs.levyHint, clubs.grossRow, clubs.levyRow, clubs.sellerNetRow) + 4 actualizadas (mySellDesc, buyOwnerDesc, buyOwnerSuccess, inbox.notif.notification.clubSold) con paridad es/en/fr/pt verificada.
- Coordinación Task 33 (pantallas desfasadas): barrido visual completo delegado en 3 subagentes paralelos (33-v1: 8 ficheros shell/auth/onboarding/dashboard/settings/bits; 33-v2: 13 ficheros markets/wallet/treasury/inbox; 33-v3: 18 ficheros control-center/competitions/tactics/squad/training/facilities/youth/staff/assistant) con tabla de mapeo estricta emerald→primary (opacidades 1:1), rose→destructive, text-white→text-primary-foreground en sólidos; amber/red/sky/violet/teal conservados como colores semánticos/datos; hex de marca de club intactos. Total ~583 conversiones en 39 ficheros — 0 residuos `emerald-|rose-` en src/components.
- Verificación curl (14 pasos): compra a 10% → price 1000/levy 100/netToSeller 900 exactos; PUT config 25% → venta de reventa 200 → levy 50/net 150 (configurable en caliente); ledger PERSONAL −1000/+900 (gross 1000, levy 100, net 900) + SYSTEM_FUND +100 INCOME_LEVY; OwnershipRecord; payload notificación {amount:1000, net:900, levyPct:10}. Mundo restaurado FK-safe tras cada fase (carteras 2801/0, SYSTEM 38629, CD Cumbre 1927 → t10_owner, Trueno CA → sistema, 0 filas residuales de ledger/ownership/notificación; qa33buyer queda como fixture documentado path OWNER wallet 0).
- E2E browser (agent-browser, sesiones t34a/t34b): vendedor ve la pista del gravamen en "Mis clubes en venta" (t34-02); obsidian renderiza monocromo y royal dorado en la misma pantalla — el barrido visual verificado en 3 temas (t34-03/t34-04); comprador ve el desglose Precio 1000/Gravamen −100/Recibe 900 en el diálogo (t34-05); compra por UI → toast "El vendedor ha recibido el importe neto" (t34-06); notificación del vendedor renderiza "vendido por 1000… Recibiste 900… (gravamen incluido)" (t34-07); dashboard final limpio sin errores de consola (t34-08). Stage Summary:

La venta de un club paga al vendedor el 90% (default) y el sistema retiene el 10%, visible y honesto en la UI (pista del vendedor + desglose del comprador + notificación) y configurable desde el Control Center en caliente.

Deuda conocida restante: pitch.tsx usa hex SVG propios (identidad del campo), html lang inicial fijo es, listas grandes sin virtualizar.

Agent: lead (Z.ai Code)
Task: "¿Por qué no funciona el reinicio del mundo?" — el endpoint /api/admin/reset era un stub honesto (auditaba pero no ejecutaba nada). Implementar el pipeline REAL backup+wipe+reseed.

Work Log:
- Diagnóstico: POST /api/admin/reset (stage confirm) devolvía {executed:false, reason:REQUIRES_BACKUP_PIPELINE} — "no data was modified" (visible en la captura del usuario). scripts/seed.ts tenía la génesis con --force pero NUNCA se cableó a la API; además su wipe era incompleto (no borraba contratos/ledger/listings/etc. → habría fallado por FK con datos vivos).
- src/lib/genesis.ts (NUEVO): módulo compartido de génesis del mundo. wipeWorld(client): borrado FK-safe de 29 tablas del mundo en orden hijos→padres (matchEvent→match→fixture→standing→cupRun→worldCupSlot→prizePool→transferRecord→bid→listing→attributeSnapshot→youth*→trainingSession→manager*→permissionGrant→staff→facility*→ledgerEntry→lineup→trainingPlan→ownershipRecord→player→club→division→region→season→fundBalance). runWorldGenesis(client,{wipe}): config+knowledge+admin+epoch+Season 1+10 regiones×10 divisiones×16 clubes+staff+instalaciones+lineups+planes+20 jugadores/club+fixtures liga+copas+40 agentes libres. Optimizado con createMany por lote (jugadores por división 320, staff/facilities/lineups/planes por región) — la reconstrucción completa tarda <20 s. Con wipe:true el epoch se FUERZA a hoy (GameDay reinicia en 1) y SYSTEM fund se resetea a 0.
- PRESERVADO por diseño (documentado en UI ×4): usuarios, sesiones, carteras personales (saldos), configuración (ConfigKey), auditoría, base de conocimiento, depósitos/retiros, notificaciones, mensajes, VPN exceptions. DESTRUIDO: clubes, jugadores, ledgers, temporadas, mercado, contratos, cantera, fondos del mundo.
- src/app/api/admin/reset/route.ts REWRITTEN: stage request igual (token 10 min). Stage confirm AHORA EJECUTA: verifica token → consume (single-use) → lock anti-reentrância (SystemState world.resetLock, TTL 15 min, 409 RESET_IN_PROGRESS) → BACKUP consistente vía `VACUUM INTO db/backups/custom-<ISO>.db` (seguro con conexiones vivas; poda dejando las 10 más recientes) → wipeWorld → runWorldGenesis → audit ADMIN_RESET_CONFIRMED/ADMIN_RESET_EXECUTED (con backupFile, counts, durationMs) o ADMIN_RESET_FAILED → devuelve {executed:true, backupFile, epochUtc, counts:{regions,divisions,clubs,players,fixtures,seasons}, durationMs}.
- scripts/seed.ts: reescrito como wrapper fino de runWorldGenesis (mismo código para CLI y API; sin duplicación).
- UI ResetCard (control-center-view.tsx): estado de éxito con CheckCircle2 tono primary — título "Mundo reconstruido desde la semilla", recuento ({clubs} clubes · {players} jugadores · {fixtures} partidos · {seasons} temporada), ruta del backup (font-mono), duración y botón "Recargar la aplicación" (window.location.reload — necesario porque el club propio del admin fue destruido). Fallback ámbar para executed:false. i18n ×4: resetAccepted (texto del stub) ELIMINADO; nuevos resetDoneTitle/Desc/Counts/Backup/Duration/Reload; resetDesc actualizado (menciona backup automático + preservación de cuentas/carteras/config). Paridad 15 claves reset ×4 verificada.
- E2E (agent-browser t35/t35b): admin login → escape hatch "Solo Centro de Control" → request token (48 chars autofilled) → confirm → panel "Mundo reconstruido" en <20 s con "1600 clubes · 32040 jugadores · 24800 partidos · 1 temporada" (t35-reset-success.png) → botón Recargar → shell OK sin Application error. Verificación DB antes/después: User 30→30, Club 1602→1600, Player 32183→32040, Region 11→10, Division 101→100, LedgerEntry 29120→0, PersonalWallet 28→28 (saldos preservados), FundBalance 14→1 (SYSTEM=0), epoch 2026-09-20→2026-09-26 (hoy), Audits +2 (CONFIRMED+EXECUTED), backup custom-2026-09-26T18-02-38-337Z.db (74 MB) en db/backups/. t10owner@knightfm.test sigue logueando (cuenta preservada) y aterriza en "Elige tu camino" (club anterior destruido, re-onboarding correcto).
- Calidad: bun run lint exit 0; bunx tsc --noEmit → 0 errores bajo src/; dev.log sin 5xx/errores en la ventana del test; sesiones de navegador cerradas.

Stage Summary:
- El reinicio del mundo YA FUNCIONA de verdad: backup SQLite consistente (VACUUM INTO) → borrado FK-safe de las 29 tablas del mundo → reconstrucción desde la semilla en <20 s → auditoría completa. Usuarios/carteras/config/auditoría se conservan; clubes/jugadores/ledgers/temporadas se destruyen y rearman (10 regiones, 100 divisiones, 1600 clubes, 32040 jugadores, epoch=hoy, fondos SYSTEM=0). Génesis unificada en src/lib/genesis.ts (CLI --force y API comparten el MISMO pipeline, sin duplicación), lock anti-doble-ejecución, backup con retención de 10, UI honesta con recuento + ruta del backup + recarga, i18n ×4.

Hallazgos prioritarios: secreto JWT con fallback constante, DB+.env en git, /api/scheduler/tick anonimo, bootstrap-email first-come, XFF suplantable, CSP unsafe-eval.
- ATAQUES EN VIVO (curl/python, confirmados):
  (1) CRITICO — JWT forjado con el fallback conocido "knight-fm-dev-secret-..." → takeover total del Control Center: GET /api/admin/config 200, PUT config economy.systemClubPrice 1000000→1, POST admin/funds acuno 1000 $Knight (cartera 2801→3801), GET /api/admin/users volco el PII de 30 usuarios. Verificacion honesta: payload/firma alterados → 401 (la firma SI se valida; el agujero era el SECRETO conocido).
  (2) HIGH — POST /api/scheduler/tick ANONIMO → 200, forzo processDueJobs (214 partidos procesados a demanda).
  (3) HIGH — hijack de admin.bootstrapEmail: armado por config + registro de hackadmin@knightfm.test → role=ADMIN con admins ya existentes (demo en vivo; usuario borrado tras la prueba).
  (4) MEDIUM — rotacion de X-Forwarded-For resetea los buckets por IP (4 IPs falsas pasaron; IP fija bloqueada en el intento 6) — explotable solo con acceso directo al :3000; detras del gateway inverso el header se sobrescribe (riesgo aceptado y documentado).
  (5) MEDIUM — mensajes: el body se persiste CRUDO ("<img src=x onerror=alert(1)>...") — sanitizeText solo limpia caracteres de control; React escapa al render (no explotable hoy), riesgo de sink no-React futuro.
  (6) LOW — duplicado concurrente de withdraw (idemKey diario) → Prisma P2002 sin mapear → 500 crudo.
  DEFENDIDOS en vivo: barrido sin token 401s; guards admin 12/12 (USER → 403 en las 12 rutas); IDOR notificaciones (lista vacia, marked:0, readAt intactos); doble-gasto 8 withdrawals paralelos al 100% del saldo (rate-limit + transaccion: cartera intacta 3801→3801); montos negativos/cero rechazados; squatting de username case-insensitive bloqueado; validacion criptografica de direccion Solana (base58 32B) rechazo direccion falsa.
- CORRECCIONES implementadas:
  (C1) CRITICO: AUTH_SECRET real (64 hex) anadido a .env; secretKey() en auth.ts ahora falla en produccion si falta (<16 chars) y usa clave aleatoria por arranque SOLO en dev. Token forjado con el secreto viejo → 401 (re-ataque verificado). Login legitimo con el nuevo secreto → 200; el flujo refresh del cliente rota sin friccion.
  (C2) scheduler/tick: exige sesion ADMIN o header x-cron-secret==CRON_SECRET (anonimo → 401 re-verificado). El rollover del dia no depende de esta ruta (/api/world/state ya dispara processDueJobs oportunisticamente).
  (C3) register: bootstrap-email SOLO concede ADMIN si adminCount===0 (primer admin); el resto via /api/admin/promote. Re-ataque: registro del email armado con admins existentes → role=USER (verificado).
  (C4) messages: < > se neutralizan a guillemets en el perimetro (re-ataque: body almacenado inofensivo).

  (C6) CSP: unsafe-eval solo en desarrollo (middleware, NODE_ENV-aware); unsafe-inline residual documentado (scripts inline de Next).

Task ID: 4-a
Agent: security-auditor-auth
Task: Auditoría estática auth/sesión/criptografía

Work Log:
- Leído worklog.md (contexto pentest, fixes previos AUTH_SECRET/admin bootstrap ya mitigados en auth.ts y register).
- Auditados línea a línea: src/lib/auth.ts (JWT/Argon2/rotación/captcha/lockout/email), los 9 endpoints /api/auth/** (login, register, refresh, logout, me, verify-email, resend-verification, password-reset/request, password-reset/confirm) + _shared.ts, src/lib/api.ts (requireAuth/rateLimit/clientIp), src/middleware.ts (perímetro, CSP, rate limit), src/lib/security.ts, src/lib/vpn.ts, src/lib/config.ts, prisma/schema.prisma (User/Session/VerificationToken), cliente src/components/auth/{store,captcha,AuthFlow}. Caddyfile y next.config.ts para topología de proxy. NO existe proxy.ts.
- Verificaciones de falsos positivos: alg confusion/"none" (jose 6 + HS256 explícito → seguro), secretos hardcodeados (ninguno; solo .env con DATABASE_URL), mass assignment register (Zod estricto, role server-side), email injection (z.email rechaza CRLF; proveedores JSON/SMTP fijos), session fixation (sin cookies de sesión, N/A), randomness (randomBytes/randomInt CSPRNG), tokens (sha256 at rest, TTL 30 min, consumo atómico updateMany con guard), Argon2id OWASP (m=19456,t=2,p=1), comparaciones === solo sobre hashes.
- Dependencias: jose@6.2.12 (JWS-only, sin advisories aplicables), @node-rs/argon2@2.2.1 OK, next@16.1.3, zod@4.3.5; next-auth@4.24.13 instalado pero con 0 imports en src/ (dependencia muerta, arrastra jose@4.x/openid-client/oauth). .env sin AUTH_SECRET (fail-closed en prod, rompe arranque) y sin EMAIL_PROVIDER (modo console por defecto → hallazgo crítico #1).
- Sin ejecución de ataques activos ni modificación de código (solo lectura).

Stage Summary:
1. CRÍTICA — password-reset/request/route.ts:42-59 (+ _shared.ts:89-105, auth.ts:187-254): sin EMAIL_PROVIDER configurado (DEFAULT y estado actual de .env), la respuesta HTTP incluye `devToken` del reset de CUALQUIER cuenta existente. Vector: POST /api/auth/password-reset/request {"email":"víctima@x","captchaToken":"x"} → devToken en JSON → confirm con token+nueva password = ATO total sin acceso al buzón. Solo requiere conocer el email (enumerable vía register 409). Fix: gate devCode/devToken con NODE_ENV!=="production" o SANDBOX_MODE=1 explícito; sin proveedor, deshabilitar el flujo de reset (503) en vez de devolver el token.
2. MEDIA — resend-verification/route.ts:39-51 y register/route.ts:153: mismo patrón `devCode` → verificación de email de cuentas ajenas conocidas (sin gate de entorno). Fix: mismo gate que #1.
3. MEDIA — api.ts:89-91 (clientIp) y middleware.ts:98-109: XFF confiado ciegamente. Caddy lo sobrescribe (header_up {remote_host}), pero el acceso directo al :3000 (next dev bindea 0.0.0.0) permite spoof → bypass de TODOS los rate limits por IP, rotación del lockout email|ip (fuerza bruta ilimitada sobre una cuenta), suplantación de IPs de la VpnException (bypass de política VPN "block") y envenenamiento del audit log. Fix: firewall al puerto de origen + lógica de "última entrada de confianza" (TRUSTED_PROXY_COUNT) o validar solo X-Real-IP fijado por el proxy de confianza.
4. MEDIA — auth.ts:94-101 rotateRefreshToken: rotación no atómica (findUnique→update) y SIN detección de reuso. Race: 2 refresh concurrentes con el mismo token → ambos obtienen accessToken (15 min) + refresh tokens; reuso de token ya rotado responde 401 sin revocar (robo indetectable). Fix: updateMany condicional (WHERE hash=old AND revokedAt IS NULL) + revocar sesión completa al recibir un hash previo ya rotado.
5. MEDIA — auth.ts:118-138 + config.ts:142: prioridad captcha invertida — security.captchaProvider tiene default "sandbox" (no vacío), por lo que el fallback a cloudflare_turnstile vía TURNSTILE_SECRET_KEY (auth.ts:120) es código muerto: el captcha es presence-only (token fijo "sandbox-knight-token", captcha.ts:61-65) aunque se configure el secret, hasta que un admin cambie la key. Sin protección anti-bot por defecto en producción. Fix: env debe activar turnstile salvo override explícito, o fail-closed si provider=sandbox && NODE_ENV=production.
6. MEDIA — api.ts:76-87: rateLimit en memoria sin tope de claves ni sweep (el middleware sí tiene KEY_CAP) con claves attacker-controlled (resend:email|ip, pwreset:email|ip, login:ip spoofable) → crecimiento ilimitado del Map = DoS memoria; igual que el Map `attempts` del lockout (auth.ts:259). Fix: cap+evict como middleware.ts:26,64-68 o unificar en el middleware.

Work Log:
